pp_river_canal_built = {
 every_neighbor_location = { limit = { topography = pp_river_shallows } save_scope_as = pp_canal_closing pp_river_canal_refresh_tile = yes }
}
pp_river_canal_destroyed = {
 save_scope_as = pp_canal_closing
 every_neighbor_location = { limit = { topography = pp_river_shallows } pp_river_canal_refresh_tile = yes }
}
